What's To Come — 4.2

U4-S2-WTC01

Start with $y=x^2$. Describe the transformations that produce $y=-3(x+1)^2-1$, including the effect of the outside factor.
Before formal instruction, record one feature of the displayed representation that could help you test your conclusion.

Teacher answer / solution

Shift horizontally by 1 unit(s) left, shift 1 unit(s) down, and apply an outside factor of -3. A useful feature should be tied to the displayed graph or structure.

Shift horizontally by 1 unit(s) left, shift 1 unit(s) down, and apply an outside factor of -3. The negative factor reflects the graph across the x-axis.

Notes Example — 4.2

U4-S2-NOTE-EX01

Teacher answer / solution

Shift horizontally by 7 unit(s) left, shift 5 unit(s) up, and apply an outside factor of -2.

Shift horizontally by 7 unit(s) left, shift 5 unit(s) up, and apply an outside factor of -2. The negative factor reflects the graph across the x-axis.

U4-S4-P2-R01

Solve the system $y=6x-3$ and $y=-5x+30$. Give the ordered-pair solution and verify it in both equations.

Teacher answer / solution

$(3,15)$.

Set the expressions for $y$ equal or eliminate a variable. The equations meet at $x=3$ and $y=15$, so the solution is $(3,15)$. Substitution satisfies both equations.

Teacher answer / solution

$(7,6)$.

U4-S5-WU1-PK03

A service has a starting fee of 9 dollars and changes by 3 dollars per use. Write a linear model and predict the total after 11 uses.

Teacher answer / solution

$C=3u+9$; after 11 uses the total is 42 dollars.

$C=3u+9$; after 11 uses the total is 42 dollars.

The quadratic $y=x^2+8x-33$ has zeros $x=-11$ and $x=3$. Explain how those zeros connect to a factored form and the graph.

Teacher answer / solution

A matching factored form is $y=(x+11)(x-3)$; the graph crosses or touches the x-axis at $(-11,0)$ and $(3,0)$.

A matching factored form is $y=(x+11)(x-3)$; the graph crosses or touches the x-axis at $(-11,0)$ and $(3,0)$.

U4-REV-Q05

Start with $y=|x|$. Describe the transformations that produce $y=-2|x-8|-9$, including the effect of the outside factor.

Teacher answer / solution

Shift horizontally by 8 unit(s) right, shift 9 unit(s) down, and apply an outside factor of -2.

Shift horizontally by 8 unit(s) right, shift 9 unit(s) down, and apply an outside factor of -2. The negative factor reflects the graph across the x-axis.

U4-REV-Q17

The quadratic $y=2x^2-6x-108$ has zeros $x=-6$ and $x=9$. Explain how those zeros connect to a factored form and the graph.

Teacher answer / solution

A matching factored form is $y=2(x+6)(x-9)$; the graph crosses or touches the x-axis at $(-6,0)$ and $(9,0)$.

A matching factored form is $y=2(x+6)(x-9)$; the graph crosses or touches the x-axis at $(-6,0)$ and $(9,0)$.
